# Lead lifecycle coverage

This page proves the pack is complete at `main` = `9955430d`. The brief's rule is that Track B is complete only when all four inventories have zero unclassified rows. They do.

## Status

| Exit criterion | State |
|---|---|
| Zero unclassified entry points, commands, field writers and routing readers at a named SHA | **Met** at `9955430d` (totals below). |
| Routing table covers every flag combination; production reconciliation explains every difference | **Met.** The table reproduces `getCallLeadReportingDecision`, `billableCallLeadFilter`, `reportingEligibleCallLeadFilter` and `planExpectedSheetTabs` over 4,800 Call and 512 Form flag combinations with 0 mismatches ([reporting-and-sheets.md](reporting-and-sheets.md)). Production differences are explained [below](#production-reconciliation). |
| Verifier sign-off with every mismatch fixed | See [Verification](#verification). |
| `form-lead.md`, `call-lead.md`, `ringcentral-call-lead-qualification.md` corrected and pointing to the pack; `docs/index.md` links it | **Met.** Corrections are listed under [Service doc corrections](#service-doc-corrections). |
| Every Owner-directive conflict listed, no code changes | **Met.** See [Owner-directive findings](#owner-directive-findings). |
| Merged through a PR with a docs-only diff | **Open.** The PR is open. Every push to `main` deploys production, so merging needs the Owner's approval. |
| Track A re-verification of `granot-effects.md` and `duplicates-and-form-fill.md` | **Open.** Sections marked "Pending re-verification against the Track A merge" describe `9955430d`. The replica lifecycle suites run after Track A merges. |

## Method

The inventories were generated at `9955430d` in the worktree, then split by path between four readers. Each reader classified every row it received and added the writers the grep missed. The coordinator classified the leftover entry points (`Coordinator` rows in Appendix A) and checked the union against the registries.

1. **Entry points.** Seeds: every `app.use` router in `src/app.ts` L52–L73, the sub-routers mounted in `src/routes/v1.routes.ts` L250–L265 and L535–L536, the 26 crons in `vercel.json`, the 5 queue consumers in `api/queues/`, and `ops/**` scripts. Every router, cron and consumer appears in Appendix A.
2. **Commands.** `git grep -hoE 'command_name: "[A-Za-z]+"' -- src ':!*.test.ts' | sort -u` gives 28 literal names. Union with the constant-defined names from `git grep -nE '(COMMAND_NAME|CommandName|COMMAND)\s*=\s*"[A-Za-z_.]+"' -- src ':!*.test.ts'`, the route-level names passed through `handleCanonicalDelete` and the create/update helpers in `v1.routes.ts`, and the distinct `command_name` values production recorded on `entity_changes` (read-only aggregate, 2026-11-20 12:37 New York, below).
3. **Field writers.** For each of the 23 lifecycle fields, a write-shaped grep (`git grep -nP` for `field:` or `field =` keys, `.field =` assignment and `$set`/`$unset`/`$inc`/`$setOnInsert` lines, over `src api ops`, excluding tests, Markdown and JSON). The grep is a superset: it also hits filters, projections and schema keys, and each hit was classified. Readers then searched by hand for spread and patch objects, aggregation pipelines, Mongoose document assignment, `bulkWrite` and `updateMany`, and added 71 rows.
4. **Routing and money readers.** The seeds from the brief, plus a sweep for `billable`, `reportingEligible`, `ReportingDecision`, `disposition`, `expectedSheetTabs`, `isDuplicate`, `excluded`, `chargeable`, CPL readers and `eligib` across `src/`.

## Production reconciliation

Read-only. Mongo `find`/`countDocuments`/`aggregate` on a secondary; Sheets `values.get` only. Sheets read 13:08:27–13:08:34 New York, Mongo 13:08:34–13:08:52, November 20, 2026. This run is after the Owner-directed bad-Form cleanup of 12:48–13:01 (see [reporting-and-sheets.md](reporting-and-sheets.md)).

Two independent computations agree exactly:
- **Code decision per document:** `planExpectedSheetTabs` and `getCallLeadReportingDecision` over every Form, Call, Booking and Cancellation (imported from the worktree at `9955430d`).
- **The documented table as a query:** the filters in [reporting-and-sheets.md](reporting-and-sheets.md), built from the code's exported `billableCallLeadFilter` and `reportingEligibleCallLeadFilter`, run as `countDocuments` at 13:14 New York.

| Disposition | Mongo Leads (code) | Mongo Leads (table query) | Tab | Mongo IDs on tab | Matched | Expected, absent | On tab, not expected |
|---|---:|---:|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Form → Forms | 7,816 | 7,816 | Forms | 7,814 | 7,811 | 5 | 3 |
| Form → Duplicate Forms | 192 | 192 | Duplicate Forms | 195 | 192 | 0 | 3 |
| Form → Bad Form Leads | 1,035 | 1,035 | Bad Form Leads | 1,040 | 1,035 | 0 | 5 |
| Form → none (`no_sync`) | 1 | 1 | — | — | — | — | — |
| Call → Calls (billable) | 1,457 | 1,457 | Calls | 1,606 | 1,402 | 55 | 204 |
| Call → Duplicate Calls | 430 | 430 | Duplicate Calls | 432 | 426 | 4 | 6 |
| Call → Bad Call Leads | 19 | 19 | Bad Call Leads | 19 | 19 | 0 | 0 |
| Call → excluded (no row planned) | 212 | 212 | — | — | — | — | (209 rows, counted above) |
| Booking → Booked Deals | 1,075 | 1,075 | Booked Deals | 1,073 | 1,072 | 3 | 1 |
| Cancellation → Cancelled Deals | 51 | 51 | Cancelled Deals | 47 | 47 | 4 | 0 |

Totals: 9,044 Form Leads, 2,118 Call Leads. Every tab row had a Mongo ID and no ID appeared twice on one tab. Call admission: 1,794 saved RingCentral qualification (1,614 `call_log_inbound_target_answered_over_120s`, 180 `call_log_inbound_target_elapsed_at_least_120s`), 69 closed Best Relocation historical inventory, 43 trusted final-import admission, 212 none.

The 212 excluded Calls hold 209 rows (203 Calls, 6 Duplicate Calls); 3 hold none. The coordinator's independent sheet-to-Mongo scan at 12:38 New York found the same 209 rows and the same anomalies (before the bad-Form cleanup, which removed the 592 bad Forms' Forms rows).

## Tests

Offline unit tests run in the worktree at `9955430d` with `node --import tsx --import ./ops/test-setup.ts --test <files>`. All cited unit files pass: R1 14 files; R2 23 files; R3 41 files (162 tests); R4 35 files (128 tests, 126 pass, 2 loopback-Mongo agreement tests skipped offline). R4's throwaway truth-table script found 0 mismatches between the filters and the decision functions over 4,800 Call combinations. Replica suites (`*.replica.test.ts`, `pnpm test:granot-lifecycle:replica`) are **not run yet**: the shared replica belongs to Track A until its release merges.

### Rules without a test (gaps)

**Form Leads (R1):** Form duplicate with an equal timestamp, or a Bad or Booked predecessor; mixed New York wall-clock versus UTC ordering against a Granot predecessor; the mark-bad guard (set and clear) on duplicate, Booked or Cancelled Forms; `noSyncOnCreate` ignoring a client `true`; a duplicate Form keeping its full CPL; a CPL correction leaving Forms at full rate with no EntityChange; clearing a bad flag without a `sheet_sync` entry; the `create_and_attach` Form's missing provenance; the `createLeadFromGranot` Form mint fields.

**Call Leads (R2):** `form_fill` never clearing after the Form is deleted, duplicate or bad; Bad Call reversal by a later final result; `fillReceiverFromRepCall` compare-and-set on an empty receiver; a manual `duplicate: false` PATCH repricing; webhook wake gating (age ≥ 120 s, ending status) in `processSessionsAndIngest`.

**Granot and Bookings (R3):** the processor mirroring only after `applied`/`linked`/`created`/`already_current`; scheduled sibling catch-up after an already-current Form; bounded never-throw scheduled post-commit steps; scheduled Record Link conflict persistence; `createBookingFromLead` re-book upsert; the cancellation mirror helpers.

**Routing and downstream (R4):** Desk ignoring RingCentral qualification; the assignment Sheet effect; the `account_agent` Desk re-sync (replica only); the Daily Operations rebuild counting unqualified or Form Fill Calls; a Bad Form priced at full CPL; the receiver-performance billable expression offline; legacy `getSummary` counting all Calls; header refusal (`sheet_identity_header_rewrite_unsafe`); an excluded Call's row left in place; a hand-removed row re-appended; formula escaping of Form Name, Email and Booked Customer Name.

## Owner-directive findings

Each finding quotes the locked rule, says what code does at `9955430d`, and proposes no code change. None softens the rule.

1. **Excluded Calls keep their Master Leads rows.** Rule (2026-11-03): only RingCentral inbound queue qualification may make a Call eligible for Calls reporting and billing; an old sheet row never qualifies it. Rule (2026-11-20): no backfill or major change on the Calls tab; the Owner handles removals there. Code: server reporting and billing exclude the 212 unadmitted Calls with no date cutoff (`callLeadReportingDisposition.ts` L18–L24); the planner plans nothing for them, so 209 pre-November-3 rows stay (203 on Calls). Consistent with both directives; listed so the Owner knows the rows are there. [call-leads.md](call-leads.md), [reporting-and-sheets.md](reporting-and-sheets.md).
2. **A hand-removed row of a billable Call comes back.** Rule (2026-11-20): the Owner handles Calls-tab removals manually. Code: `batchWriter.ts` L145–L154 appends when the Mongo ID is missing, so a removed row of a Call that still routes to Calls returns on its next Granot update, booking, cancellation, Form Fill change, receiver fill or final result. 55 billable Calls and 4 Form Fill Calls are in that state now. Manual removal sticks only for excluded Calls.
3. **Scheduled Granot runs do not catch a Call up from an already-current Form.** Rule (2026-10-28/29): a Granot update to a Form Lead mirrors job, priority and receiver onto the same customer's qualified Call. Code: `granotHttpCollector/scheduledLeadChange.ts` L153–L162 and L227–L235 mirror only after an applied Form change; the webhook processor also mirrors after `already_current` (`granotLifecycle/processor.ts` L240–L258). Zero current impact (42 eligible jobless Calls in 30 days, none waiting). **Track A is fixing this.**
4. **The retired Best Relocation sheet path is still reachable for Bookings.** Rule (2026-11-03): external ingestion remains retired. Code: `POST /api/v1/booked-leads/from-source` accepts `ingestion_source: "best_relocation_sheet"` (`validation/v1/bookings.validation.ts` L87) and `existingWrites.ts:runExistingCreateBookedLeadFromSource` L639–L677 does not strip it, unlike Form create, leadless Booking and cancellation. The resolver then matches a Best Relocation Call by job or phone and rewrites its phone and job with no eligibility check (`bookings/bookingSourceResolver.ts` L39–L98). It creates no Call.
5. **"Booking intake prefers the Form Lead" applies only to Employee Booking Submissions.** Rule (2026-10-29). Code: only `employeeBookings/leadMatchEvaluator.ts:preferFormLeadForSharedJob` L292–L313 applies it. `confirmGranotBooking`, the production path, attaches the Granot identity target with no Form-over-Call check (`granotLifecycle/confirmAttachment.ts` L34–L57); Owner resolve paths and `connectBookingToLead` apply none. No current instance measured.
6. **Desk eligibility does not read Call admission.** Rule (2026-11-03): only qualified Calls take part in the lifecycle (restated 2026-11-20). Code: `salesOutreach/subjects/eligibility.ts` L39–L53 and `leadFacts.ts` L56–L86 never check `hasReportingEligibleCall`. Latent: 0 affected subjects at 12:47 New York. The Daily Operations rebuild and legacy Analytics summary/source/geographic reports also count unqualified Calls (`dailyOperations/rebuild.ts` L226–L252 and L399–L438; `analytics/summary.service.ts` L15; `analyticsFilters.ts` L91–L110). [downstream-consumers.md](downstream-consumers.md).
7. **Glossary: Ingestion Origin and Ingested Contact Snapshot.** Rule (CONTEXT.md): every Lead gets a server-assigned origin and an immutable contact snapshot at creation. Code: `employeeBookings/bookingLeadAttachment.service.ts:createAndAttachReconciliationFormLead` L165–L175 sets neither. Never run in production.
8. **Glossary: Form duplicate.** Rule (CONTEXT.md): a Form is a Duplicate Lead when its Source Granularity already has an eligible non-duplicate Form with the same phone or email. Code: `granotLifecycle/createLeadFromGranot.ts:createGranotFormLead` L581–L739 never runs `findDuplicateFormLeadMatch`; and WordPress/Admin Forms store New York wall-clock time as UTC while Granot Forms store real UTC, so "earlier" is off by 4–5 hours across origins (`formLead.service.ts` L356, `createLeadFromGranot.ts` L715, `duplicateLead.service.ts` L104–L117).
9. **Live lead cost.** Workspace note (2026-10-06): Analytics prices every lead live, "no stamped-`cpl` sums". Code: legacy Analytics lead cost and receiver performance still sum stamped `cpl` (`analytics/leadCost.service.ts` L46–L138, `receiverAgentPerformance.service.ts` L135–L184). Only `src/services/insights/**` prices live. The glossary's "Duplicate Call Leads receive zero CPL" is also loose: stamped `cpl` is not zeroed when a Call later becomes a repeat or Form Fill; readers price it at zero.

### Open questions for the Owner (not conflicts)

- Should Bad Forms be billable? Insights prices a non-duplicate Bad Form at full live CPL (`insights/facts.ts` L139–L140); no directive covers it.
- Should a Bad Form make a Call a Form Fill? `hasFormFillForCallLead` excludes only duplicate Forms.
- `form_fill` never clears when its Form is deleted, becomes a duplicate or becomes bad. Intended?
- The overnight rule matches only a `Missed` final result. Overnight `Voicemail` Calls of 120 s or more stay billable. Should they count as Bad Calls? (No production Call is bad by the overnight rule today: 1,693 of 1,794 qualified Calls predate stored start times and final results.)
- Form Fill and Bad Calls still start a 90-day repeat window for the caller's next Call. Confirm.
- Admin `PATCH /api/v1/call-leads/:id` can set or clear the 90-day `duplicate` flag by hand (one production use, September 3). Keep?
- A Form whose `duplicate` flag flips keeps its old Forms or Duplicate Forms row. Keep that asymmetry?
- A Bad Form that is later booked or cancelled can never be unmarked; a trusted Best Relocation review has no reversal path.
- CPL Corrections write no EntityChange and no revision, so Outreach and the Job timeline cannot see them.
- `createBookingFromLead` has no Lead state guard and re-booking upserts the old Booking in place, leaving `cancelled` set. `confirmGranotBooking` does not check `created_on_unmatched`.
- Desk reassignment (`sales_outreach_assignment`) rewrites the Sales Rep cell value without a Sheet intent, so the cell updates only at the Lead's next delivery.
- Production WordPress resubmissions are not deduplicated by receipt (receipts are written only in test mode); the duplicate rule usually catches them.
- Two unwired writers (`syncCallLeadEnrichment`, `syncBookedCallLeadReconciliation`) still write Call fields outside canonical commands. Fence or delete them in a code change.
- `backfillDeskReceiverFromGranot` (9 production rows, October 5) has no tracked code at any commit.
- Production residue: 3 certification fixture Bookings, 28 orphan August Cancellations, 5 Form rows and 1 Booked Deals row whose records no longer exist.

## Service doc corrections

- **`docs/knowledge/services/form-lead.md`** (11 edits, R1): the PATCH route runs `updateSourceOwnedLead`; Best Relocation sheet ingestion and `reconcileExternalSheetReviewV1` are historical; the trusted `local` pass-through is unreachable; Form Fill candidates must be reporting-eligible, the inline pass covers 25 and `form_fill_work` the rest; `duplicate_zero` CPL is for Calls only (176 duplicate Forms are at full `resolved` rate); the mark-bad guard also refuses clearing; Form Fill is rescheduled by Form changes and not undone by delete; `create_and_attach` assigns no origin; `deleteFormLead` is the delete command; a trusted review's note prints before the canonical reason in the Bad Lead cell.
- **`docs/knowledge/services/call-lead.md`** (7 edits, R2): removed `bestRelocationFinalCall.ts` from `applies_to`; the final-import command is past tense; the 90-day window is `[t − 90 d, t)` and earlier-only within the Source Granularity; the Form Fill scope and that Bad Forms count; the durable `form_fill_work` drain and that `form_fill` never clears; `fillReceiverAgentFromAnsweredCall` was removed in `9007f43f`.
- **`docs/knowledge/services/ringcentral-call-lead-qualification.md`** (6 edits and 1 annotation, R2): an already-processed call in create mode first records a newer final result; Granot Call creation is historical; the final Best Relocation command and `generalCallRun.ts` are removed; the real log event names; the watermark-only update is a plain transactional `updateOne` outside the canonical command.

Other stale docs found but not edited (outside the brief's correction list): `granot-lifecycle/identity.md` L49 (mirror gate omits reporting eligibility), `granot-lifecycle/automation-apply.md` L37, `employee-bookings.md` L60 (Form preference), `cancellation-mirror.md` L33/L80, `bookings.md` L224, `google-sheets.md` L73/L82–L88 and `sheet-sync.md` L191–L210 (formula cells are managed since PR #237; duplicate flips keep the old primary row), `sales-outreach-lifecycle.md` L51/L95, `insights.md` L45–L49.

## Regenerating this page

The reader TSVs and the build script live outside the repository. To re-check at a new SHA, rerun the four inventory commands in [Method](#method), diff the hit lists against Appendices A–D, and classify every new hit. A new unclassified hit blocks completion.

## Appendix A. Entry points
